Leaked scratch dirs are individually tiny — 449,259 of them measured 2.6 GiB total — so every byte-based probe reads this as noise. The cost is that a directory with hundreds of thousands of dirents degrades every mkdir/readdir/stat the fleet performs under the shared TMPDIR, including the testcontainer start-lock. That was tied to spurious integration-test timeouts and elevated host load fleet-wide (2026-07-09).\n\nDOMINANT NAME SHAPES (random suffixes collapsed — the top line is almost always the culprit):\n   2857 live-fed-gate-snapshot.<rand>\n   1942 papercusp-affected-tests-<n>-<hex>.log\n   1107 prov-sidecar-<rand>\n    739 pc-heavy-start.<rand>\n    432 sidecar-fresh-<rand>\n\nHOW TO ACT ON THIS:\n1. Take the top shape above and find who creates it: grep -rn '<the literal prefix>' --include=*.ts --include=*.mjs --include=*.sh . | grep -v node_modules\n2. Read that creation site's cleanup. If the cleanup is a 'process.on(\"exit\")' handler, IT IS DEAD CODE under vitest: vitest-config.ts pins pool:'forks' and tinypool SIGNAL-KILLS each per-file worker, so Node never runs exit handlers there. Falsify it in both directions before believing either result — run the module under 'tsx -e' (a normal exit, cleanup runs) and under 'npm run test:file' with an isolated TMPDIR (a signal kill, cleanup does not).\n3. The durable fix shape is in libs/test-config/src/hermetic-tmpdir.ts: NEST per-process scratch under one parent so a leak costs the shared TMPDIR one top-level entry, and SWEEP siblings whose creating pid is dead on create. Cleanup that requires the dying process to cooperate is not cleanup — a SIGKILLed process runs nothing.\n4. Reclaiming is safe only with an age floor, so a live run's scratch is never deleted under it: find '/tmp' -maxdepth 1 -mindepth 1 -type d -name '<shape>*' -mmin +240 -exec rm -rf {} +\n\nWHY BOTH /tmp AND /tmp/pcv ARE WATCHED: vitest-config.ts FORCES TMPDIR=/tmp/pcv for every test process, so a count of /tmp's top level cannot see the directory where test scratch actually lands. Measuring only /tmp under-reported this exact leak by 6x (74,385 seen vs 523,644 real). If a new tool forces its own TMPDIR, add it to BACKUP_TMPDIR_WATCH.\n\nThis probe is detection-only; it never deletes anything.","state":"open","assignee":"su-20055aba-cfaa-4571-912b-47808d3c938a","takenAt":"2026-10-14T10:57:43.008Z","lastProgressAt":"2026-10-14T13:45:11.976Z","assignedBy":null,"createdBy":"su-loopback","severity":"critical","goalId":null,"tags":null,"sourcePlanSlug":null,"sourcePlanItemIds":null,"redundancy":null,"expectedCostCents":null,"parent":null,"payload":{"_ei":{"scope":"harness:papercusp","source":"engineer","severity":"critical","created_by":"su-loopback","signal_origin":"organic"},"tags":["backup","data-safety","host-health"],"paths":["libs/test-config/src/hermetic-tmpdir.ts"],"dedupCoverage":{"lexical":"ok","degraded":true,"semantic":"unavailable"},"admissionIdentity":{"titleKey":"title:7865 entries has leaking main many precision scratch server shared something tmp tmpdir too tower","schemaVersion":"admission-identity-v1"},"freshnessEnvelope":{"observedAt":"2026-08-27T12:00:15.011Z","linkedFixRefs":[],"reporterSession":"su-loopback","observedSourceSha":"5bb09b0c0f","observedRuntimeSha":"5bb09b0c0f","deployBuildIdentity":"5bb09b0c0f@0.0.0","reporterRuntimeVersion":"0.0.0"},"claim_history_post_id":9001788375295},"rank":null,"rankWriter":null,"rankUpdatedAt":null,"priority":null,"terminalOwner":null,"terminalCompletionRef":null,"terminalCompletionEvidence":null,"completionAuthority":null,"origin":"local","auditVerdict":null,"verifiedAuthorGithubUserId":null,"createdAt":"2026-08-27T12:00:15.011Z","updatedAt":"2026-10-14T10:57:43.011Z","closedAt":null},"holder":{"goalRef":"WI-75008","goalText":null,"assumptions":{"count":1,"keys":["launch-provenance"]},"declaredAt":"2026-10-14 06:57:43.008579-04","stale":true,"agreement":"sole","competing":[]},"checkpoint":"## Did\nReconciled and acknowledged the leader's 13:42Z message.
